        Idea:
ArtAgents is a file-based, single-host Python CLI for running creative pipelines (video/audio/image) that mix code, AI, and human steps. Today it has a pack-based plugin architecture, orchestrator + executor schemas (python/command runtime kinds only), project runs under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/, threads with provenance, and a CLI gateway. We are extending it so an agent can be put into 'task mode' and walked through a frozen plan that mixes code, AI, and human steps, with iteration loops and fan-out.

NON-NEGOTIABLES:
- Single-host, file-based. No daemon, no web server.
- Honor-based with strong logging. We do not pretend to sandbox a misaligned agent.
- Migration is additive. Existing JSON manifests and python/command runtime kinds keep working.
- Existing pack mechanism stays.

V1 DESIGN (post-critique, trimmed):

THREE STEP KINDS:
- code: deterministic argv, runner subprocesses, returncode + produces.
- attested: agent or human task. instructions + produces + ack records identity (agent_id or actor pinned via ARTAGENTS_ACTOR) + evidence.
- nested: delegates to a child plan; gate sees the structure for hashing/pinning.

ITERATION AND FAN-OUT ARE BODY ATTRIBUTES, NOT KINDS:
- repeat: {until: user_approves|verifier_passes|quorum, max_iterations, on_exhaust}
- repeat: {for_each: <input_set>}
Both apply to any step or to a body block in a nested plan.

PRODUCES WITH INLINE CHECKS (replaces separate verifier substep):
  produces = {
    'audio': file(path='audio.wav', check=audio_duration_min(30)),
    'notes': json_file(path='notes.json', schema='notes.schema.json', min_items=3),
  }
The gate runs the check; failure rewinds the cursor. passes_test is a regular code step.

STORAGE (under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/):
- <slug>/active_run.json -- pointer to in-flight run + plan hash
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/plan.json -- immutable, hash-pinned (renamed from 'checklist' since it's a tree)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/events.jsonl -- append-only, plain hash-chained (prev_hash field), sole truth
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md -- contract for whoever's driving
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/ -- artifact files (or symlinks into .cas)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/iterations/NNN/ -- when repeat:until is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/items/<item-id>/ -- when repeat:for_each is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/inbox/ -- external completion signals get dropped here
- <slug>/.cas/<sha256> -- per-project CAS for v1 (not shared)

NO crypto: hash chain is plain sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json). Tripwire for accidental edits, not protection against malicious users. We do not need HMAC or key handles.

NO .index.db in v1. find + grep on events.jsonl is fine until grep is slow.

GATE (above dispatch, single function):
- active_run.json exists for this project?
- plan.json hash matches the pin in active_run.json?
- events.jsonl hash chain intact?
- incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command?
If any fails, reject with the exact recovery command.

LIFECYCLE VERBS, scoped by audience:
artagents next | ack | status | abort                                  (agent-facing, mid-run)
artagents start | abort | status | runs ls                             (operator)
artagents author new | check | describe | test | compile | explain     (author)

ACK DECISIONS (intentionally distinct):
--decision approve              -- advance cursor
--decision retry                -- only valid after verifier failure; rerun body, stderr is feedback
--decision iterate --feedback   -- only valid when repeat.until=user_approves; appends to cumulative constraints
--decision abort                -- end run

ACK requires --agent <id> for attested steps with attestor_type=agent, or --actor <name> matching ARTAGENTS_ACTOR for attestor_type=human. Self-acks and unpinned actors are rejected.

For repeat:for_each: --item <id> targets a specific item; partial approval is supported.

NUDGE (out-of-process, optional, Claude Code only for v1):
A Stop hook runs artagents next. The next output ALWAYS includes the prohibition preamble verbatim, not just on first call -- this is the re-injection mechanism that fights context decay.

AUTHORING:
- Python DSL artagents.orchestrate is the ONLY committed representation. JSON manifest is generated at author compile into a build/ dir (gitignored) and that's what the runtime hashes/pins.
- Verifier helper library artagents.verify: file_nonempty, json_schema, json_file, audio_duration_min, image_dimensions, all_of.
- Pack layout: <pack>/<orch>.py (committed), <pack>/build/<orch>.json (gitignored), <pack>/fixtures/<name>/, <pack>/golden/<name>.events.jsonl.
- author check is sub-second static validation: schema, every produces/requires reference resolves, every nested plan resolves, attested steps with non-trivial produces have semantic checks (sentinel-existence-only is rejected at definition time).
- author describe pretty-prints the DAG.
- author test --fixture runs --dry-run --auto-approve, diffs events.jsonl against golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl.

V1 BUILD ORDER (recommended phases):
1. Kernel: events.jsonl + plain hash chain + plan hash pin + gate above dispatch + active_run.json. Runner becomes dumb; gate is a single decorated function above runner dispatch.
2. Three step kinds: code, attested, nested. (code is a strict superset of today's python+command runtime kinds.)
3. produces-with-inline-checks; repeat:{until} and repeat:{for_each} on bodies.
4. Authoring: Python DSL + verify helpers + author check + author describe + author new.
5. Lifecycle verbs split by audience as above.
6. Stop-hook nudge with mandatory prohibition preamble.
7. CAS per-project (under <slug>/.cas/), symlink-based produces.
8. Inbox surface for external completion signals.
9. Author test with golden runs.

EXISTING REPO STATE:
- artagents/core/orchestrator/ exists with schema.py, runner.py, registry.py, cli.py, api.py. Currently knows ORCHESTRATOR_KINDS={built_in, external} and RUNTIME_KINDS={python, command}. OrchestratorPlan / OrchestratorPlanStep skeletons exist (used for dry-run output).
- artagents/core/project/ exists with run lifecycle (prepare_project_run / finalize_project_run), paths.py with DEFAULT_PROJECTS_ROOT.
- artagents/threads/ exists with thread_wrapper around runs, @active concept.
- artagents/packs/ holds builtin packs (cut, transcribe, human_notes, open_in_reigh, etc.).
- Migration to packs is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13).

ASSUMED OPEN-CALL DEFAULTS (the design plan should call these out and confirm or revise):
- Keep nested step kind as data (gate sees the structure); forbid code-step-calls-orchestrator.
- Python DSL is the only committed representation; JSON manifest is a build artifact in gitignored build/ dirs.
- Drop .index.db from v1; ship with file-walk verbs.

DELIVERABLE EXPECTATIONS for the design doc:
- Concrete phasing aligned to build order 1-9 above; each phase has scope, files touched, breaking-change risk classification (additive vs breaking), and exit criteria.
- Test strategy at each phase (golden runs are the regression format).
- Migration of one builtin pack (suggest hype.cut or transcribe) to the new shape as the canonical example, including the diff shape.
- Documentation deliverables identified: AUTHORING.md (one-page reference for both human and LLM authors), AGENT.md template, README.md updates.
- Risk register (single-host assumption, semantic verifier discipline, context decay re-injection, agent honor model boundary).
- Cut points where work can stop and still ship something useful (e.g., after phase 3, after phase 5).
- A ## Settled Decisions section at the bottom listing every load-bearing design call (SD-NNN format) so future code-mode runs can inherit them via --from-doc.

```json
{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md\n+---\n+title: \"Orchestrator V1 Plan\"\n+mode: \"doc / metaplan\"\n+status: \"draft\"\n+scope: \"task-mode orchestrator runtime and authoring plan\"\n+---\n+\n+# Orchestrator V1 Plan\n+\n+## 1. Executive Summary\n+\n+ArtAgents V1 adds task mode: a single-host, file-based way to put an agent inside a frozen plan and advance it through creative pipeline work one permitted command at a time. The plan can mix code, AI, and human steps without introducing a daemon, web server, or pretend sandbox. Runtime behavior collapses to three step kinds: `code` for deterministic argv subprocesses, `attested` for agent or human work that requires identity-pinned acknowledgment and evidence, and `nested` for child-plan delegation whose structure remains visible to the gate for hashing and pinning. Iteration and fan-out are not separate kinds; they are body attributes that can wrap any step or nested block through `repeat.until` and `repeat.for_each`. Migration stays additive: existing pack mechanics, JSON manifests, and `python` / `command` runtime kinds keep working while the new Python DSL compiles hash-pinned task plans for V1 runs.\n+\n+## 2. Goals & Non-Goals\n+\n+### Goals\n+\n+- Freeze each task-mode run around an immutable, hash-pinned `plan.json` so the agent and operator share one durable execution contract.\n+- Put a gate above dispatch that validates the active run pointer, plan hash, event chain, and next-command match before any runner side effects occur.\n+- Reduce task-mode execution to three step kinds: `code`, `attested`, and `nested`.\n+- Model iteration and fan-out as `repeat` body attributes rather than additional step kinds.\n+- Use golden-run `events.jsonl` files as the regression format for author tests.\n+- Split lifecycle verbs by audience: agent-facing mid-run verbs, operator run-control verbs, and authoring verbs.\n+- Preserve the existing pack mechanism and keep current orchestrator manifests and runtime kinds loadable during migration.\n+\n+### Non-Goals\n+\n+- No daemon.\n+- No web server.\n+- No HMAC, keyed crypto, or key-handle scheme in V1.\n+- No `.index.db`; file walking and `grep` over `events.jsonl` are sufficient until proven slow.\n+- No shared CAS; V1 uses only a per-project `.cas/` store.\n+- No sandbox against malicious agents. V1 is honor-based with strong logging and tripwires for accidental edits, not a confinement system.\n*** End Patch\n"
}
```

        docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md

        Idea:
ArtAgents is a file-based, single-host Python CLI for running creative pipelines (video/audio/image) that mix code, AI, and human steps. Today it has a pack-based plugin architecture, orchestrator + executor schemas (python/command runtime kinds only), project runs under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/, threads with provenance, and a CLI gateway. We are extending it so an agent can be put into 'task mode' and walked through a frozen plan that mixes code, AI, and human steps, with iteration loops and fan-out.

NON-NEGOTIABLES:
- Single-host, file-based. No daemon, no web server.
- Honor-based with strong logging. We do not pretend to sandbox a misaligned agent.
- Migration is additive. Existing JSON manifests and python/command runtime kinds keep working.
- Existing pack mechanism stays.

V1 DESIGN (post-critique, trimmed):

THREE STEP KINDS:
- code: deterministic argv, runner subprocesses, returncode + produces.
- attested: agent or human task. instructions + produces + ack records identity (agent_id or actor pinned via ARTAGENTS_ACTOR) + evidence.
- nested: delegates to a child plan; gate sees the structure for hashing/pinning.

ITERATION AND FAN-OUT ARE BODY ATTRIBUTES, NOT KINDS:
- repeat: {until: user_approves|verifier_passes|quorum, max_iterations, on_exhaust}
- repeat: {for_each: <input_set>}
Both apply to any step or to a body block in a nested plan.

PRODUCES WITH INLINE CHECKS (replaces separate verifier substep):
  produces = {
    'audio': file(path='audio.wav', check=audio_duration_min(30)),
    'notes': json_file(path='notes.json', schema='notes.schema.json', min_items=3),
  }
The gate runs the check; failure rewinds the cursor. passes_test is a regular code step.

STORAGE (under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/):
- <slug>/active_run.json -- pointer to in-flight run + plan hash
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/plan.json -- immutable, hash-pinned (renamed from 'checklist' since it's a tree)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/events.jsonl -- append-only, plain hash-chained (prev_hash field), sole truth
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md -- contract for whoever's driving
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/ -- artifact files (or symlinks into .cas)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/iterations/NNN/ -- when repeat:until is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/items/<item-id>/ -- when repeat:for_each is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/inbox/ -- external completion signals get dropped here
- <slug>/.cas/<sha256> -- per-project CAS for v1 (not shared)

NO crypto: hash chain is plain sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json). Tripwire for accidental edits, not protection against malicious users. We do not need HMAC or key handles.

NO .index.db in v1. find + grep on events.jsonl is fine until grep is slow.

GATE (above dispatch, single function):
- active_run.json exists for this project?
- plan.json hash matches the pin in active_run.json?
- events.jsonl hash chain intact?
- incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command?
If any fails, reject with the exact recovery command.

LIFECYCLE VERBS, scoped by audience:
artagents next | ack | status | abort                                  (agent-facing, mid-run)
artagents start | abort | status | runs ls                             (operator)
artagents author new | check | describe | test | compile | explain     (author)

ACK DECISIONS (intentionally distinct):
--decision approve              -- advance cursor
--decision retry                -- only valid after verifier failure; rerun body, stderr is feedback
--decision iterate --feedback   -- only valid when repeat.until=user_approves; appends to cumulative constraints
--decision abort                -- end run

ACK requires --agent <id> for attested steps with attestor_type=agent, or --actor <name> matching ARTAGENTS_ACTOR for attestor_type=human. Self-acks and unpinned actors are rejected.

For repeat:for_each: --item <id> targets a specific item; partial approval is supported.

NUDGE (out-of-process, optional, Claude Code only for v1):
A Stop hook runs artagents next. The next output ALWAYS includes the prohibition preamble verbatim, not just on first call -- this is the re-injection mechanism that fights context decay.

AUTHORING:
- Python DSL artagents.orchestrate is the ONLY committed representation. JSON manifest is generated at author compile into a build/ dir (gitignored) and that's what the runtime hashes/pins.
- Verifier helper library artagents.verify: file_nonempty, json_schema, json_file, audio_duration_min, image_dimensions, all_of.
- Pack layout: <pack>/<orch>.py (committed), <pack>/build/<orch>.json (gitignored), <pack>/fixtures/<name>/, <pack>/golden/<name>.events.jsonl.
- author check is sub-second static validation: schema, every produces/requires reference resolves, every nested plan resolves, attested steps with non-trivial produces have semantic checks (sentinel-existence-only is rejected at definition time).
- author describe pretty-prints the DAG.
- author test --fixture runs --dry-run --auto-approve, diffs events.jsonl against golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl.

V1 BUILD ORDER (recommended phases):
1. Kernel: events.jsonl + plain hash chain + plan hash pin + gate above dispatch + active_run.json. Runner becomes dumb; gate is a single decorated function above runner dispatch.
2. Three step kinds: code, attested, nested. (code is a strict superset of today's python+command runtime kinds.)
3. produces-with-inline-checks; repeat:{until} and repeat:{for_each} on bodies.
4. Authoring: Python DSL + verify helpers + author check + author describe + author new.
5. Lifecycle verbs split by audience as above.
6. Stop-hook nudge with mandatory prohibition preamble.
7. CAS per-project (under <slug>/.cas/), symlink-based produces.
8. Inbox surface for external completion signals.
9. Author test with golden runs.

EXISTING REPO STATE:
- artagents/core/orchestrator/ exists with schema.py, runner.py, registry.py, cli.py, api.py. Currently knows ORCHESTRATOR_KINDS={built_in, external} and RUNTIME_KINDS={python, command}. OrchestratorPlan / OrchestratorPlanStep skeletons exist (used for dry-run output).
- artagents/core/project/ exists with run lifecycle (prepare_project_run / finalize_project_run), paths.py with DEFAULT_PROJECTS_ROOT.
- artagents/threads/ exists with thread_wrapper around runs, @active concept.
- artagents/packs/ holds builtin packs (cut, transcribe, human_notes, open_in_reigh, etc.).
- Migration to packs is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13).

ASSUMED OPEN-CALL DEFAULTS (the design plan should call these out and confirm or revise):
- Keep nested step kind as data (gate sees the structure); forbid code-step-calls-orchestrator.
- Python DSL is the only committed representation; JSON manifest is a build artifact in gitignored build/ dirs.
- Drop .index.db from v1; ship with file-walk verbs.

DELIVERABLE EXPECTATIONS for the design doc:
- Concrete phasing aligned to build order 1-9 above; each phase has scope, files touched, breaking-change risk classification (additive vs breaking), and exit criteria.
- Test strategy at each phase (golden runs are the regression format).
- Migration of one builtin pack (suggest hype.cut or transcribe) to the new shape as the canonical example, including the diff shape.
- Documentation deliverables identified: AUTHORING.md (one-page reference for both human and LLM authors), AGENT.md template, README.md updates.
- Risk register (single-host assumption, semantic verifier discipline, context decay re-injection, agent honor model boundary).
- Cut points where work can stop and still ship something useful (e.g., after phase 3, after phase 5).
- A ## Settled Decisions section at the bottom listing every load-bearing design call (SD-NNN format) so future code-mode runs can inherit them via --from-doc.

tool_use apply_patch
```json
{
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md\n@@\n ### Non-Goals\n \n - No daemon.\n - No web server.\n - No HMAC, keyed crypto, or key-handle scheme in V1.\n - No `.index.db`; file walking and `grep` over `events.jsonl` are sufficient until proven slow.\n - No shared CAS; V1 uses only a per-project `.cas/` store.\n - No sandbox against malicious agents. V1 is honor-based with strong logging and tripwires for accidental edits, not a confinement system.\n+\n+## 3. Data Model\n+\n+Task-mode state lives under the existing projects root, `~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/`, scoped by project slug:\n+\n+```text\n+<slug>/\n+  active_run.json\n+  runs/\n+    <run-id>/\n+      plan.json\n+      events.jsonl\n+      AGENT.md\n+      steps/\n+        <step-id>/\n+          produces/\n+          iterations/\n+            NNN/\n+          items/\n+            <item-id>/\n+      inbox/\n+  .cas/\n+    <sha256>\n+```\n+\n+`active_run.json` is the small mutable pointer for an in-flight task-mode run:\n+\n+```json\n+{\"run_id\": \"<run-id>\", \"plan_hash\": \"sha256:<hex>\"}\n+```\n+\n+`runs/<run-id>/plan.json` is immutable after `artagents start`. It is the runtime tree generated by `author compile`, hash-pinned by `active_run.json`, and deliberately named `plan.json` rather than `checklist` because the structure contains nested bodies, fan-out, and repeat loops. The existing `OrchestratorPlan` / `OrchestratorPlanStep` dataclasses in `artagents/core/orchestrator/runner.py` remain the dry-run display shape for current orchestrators. V1 should extend or adapt that shape into the compiled task-mode tree rather than treating it as another source of truth; the pinned `plan.json` is what the gate reads.\n+\n+`runs/<run-id>/events.jsonl` is append-only and is the sole truth for task-mode progress. Each event stores `prev_hash`; the next hash is plain `sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json)`. The chain is a tripwire for accidental edits and partial writes, not crypto against a malicious local user. V1 therefore does not add HMAC, keyed signing, key handles, or any claim of tamper-proof storage.\n+\n+Step artifacts are written under `steps/<step-id>/produces/`. `iterations/NNN/` exists only for `repeat.until` bodies, and `items/<item-id>/` exists only for `repeat.for_each` bodies so partial fan-out approval can be addressed without inventing separate run records. `inbox/` is the file-drop surface for external completion signals. `.cas/<sha256>` is per-project storage only; produced files may later become symlinks into it.\n+\n+There is no `.index.db` in V1. Status, audit, and author-test tooling should derive state by reading `active_run.json`, validating the `events.jsonl` chain, and walking files under the run directory.\n*** End Patch\n"
}
```

> DEVELOPER

Author the planned document sections.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents

        Output path (write all sections here):
        docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md

        Idea:
ArtAgents is a file-based, single-host Python CLI for running creative pipelines (video/audio/image) that mix code, AI, and human steps. Today it has a pack-based plugin architecture, orchestrator + executor schemas (python/command runtime kinds only), project runs under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/, threads with provenance, and a CLI gateway. We are extending it so an agent can be put into 'task mode' and walked through a frozen plan that mixes code, AI, and human steps, with iteration loops and fan-out.

NON-NEGOTIABLES:
- Single-host, file-based. No daemon, no web server.
- Honor-based with strong logging. We do not pretend to sandbox a misaligned agent.
- Migration is additive. Existing JSON manifests and python/command runtime kinds keep working.
- Existing pack mechanism stays.

V1 DESIGN (post-critique, trimmed):

THREE STEP KINDS:
- code: deterministic argv, runner subprocesses, returncode + produces.
- attested: agent or human task. instructions + produces + ack records identity (agent_id or actor pinned via ARTAGENTS_ACTOR) + evidence.
- nested: delegates to a child plan; gate sees the structure for hashing/pinning.

ITERATION AND FAN-OUT ARE BODY ATTRIBUTES, NOT KINDS:
- repeat: {until: user_approves|verifier_passes|quorum, max_iterations, on_exhaust}
- repeat: {for_each: <input_set>}
Both apply to any step or to a body block in a nested plan.

PRODUCES WITH INLINE CHECKS (replaces separate verifier substep):
  produces = {
    'audio': file(path='audio.wav', check=audio_duration_min(30)),
    'notes': json_file(path='notes.json', schema='notes.schema.json', min_items=3),
  }
The gate runs the check; failure rewinds the cursor. passes_test is a regular code step.

STORAGE (under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/):
- <slug>/active_run.json -- pointer to in-flight run + plan hash
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/plan.json -- immutable, hash-pinned (renamed from 'checklist' since it's a tree)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/events.jsonl -- append-only, plain hash-chained (prev_hash field), sole truth
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md -- contract for whoever's driving
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/ -- artifact files (or symlinks into .cas)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/iterations/NNN/ -- when repeat:until is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/items/<item-id>/ -- when repeat:for_each is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/inbox/ -- external completion signals get dropped here
- <slug>/.cas/<sha256> -- per-project CAS for v1 (not shared)

NO crypto: hash chain is plain sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json). Tripwire for accidental edits, not protection against malicious users. We do not need HMAC or key handles.

NO .index.db in v1. find + grep on events.jsonl is fine until grep is slow.

GATE (above dispatch, single function):
- active_run.json exists for this project?
- plan.json hash matches the pin in active_run.json?
- events.jsonl hash chain intact?
- incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command?
If any fails, reject with the exact recovery command.

LIFECYCLE VERBS, scoped by audience:
artagents next | ack | status | abort                                  (agent-facing, mid-run)
artagents start | abort | status | runs ls                             (operator)
artagents author new | check | describe | test | compile | explain     (author)

ACK DECISIONS (intentionally distinct):
--decision approve              -- advance cursor
--decision retry                -- only valid after verifier failure; rerun body, stderr is feedback
--decision iterate --feedback   -- only valid when repeat.until=user_approves; appends to cumulative constraints
--decision abort                -- end run

ACK requires --agent <id> for attested steps with attestor_type=agent, or --actor <name> matching ARTAGENTS_ACTOR for attestor_type=human. Self-acks and unpinned actors are rejected.

For repeat:for_each: --item <id> targets a specific item; partial approval is supported.

NUDGE (out-of-process, optional, Claude Code only for v1):
A Stop hook runs artagents next. The next output ALWAYS includes the prohibition preamble verbatim, not just on first call -- this is the re-injection mechanism that fights context decay.

AUTHORING:
- Python DSL artagents.orchestrate is the ONLY committed representation. JSON manifest is generated at author compile into a build/ dir (gitignored) and that's what the runtime hashes/pins.
- Verifier helper library artagents.verify: file_nonempty, json_schema, json_file, audio_duration_min, image_dimensions, all_of.
- Pack layout: <pack>/<orch>.py (committed), <pack>/build/<orch>.json (gitignored), <pack>/fixtures/<name>/, <pack>/golden/<name>.events.jsonl.
- author check is sub-second static validation: schema, every produces/requires reference resolves, every nested plan resolves, attested steps with non-trivial produces have semantic checks (sentinel-existence-only is rejected at definition time).
- author describe pretty-prints the DAG.
- author test --fixture runs --dry-run --auto-approve, diffs events.jsonl against golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl.

V1 BUILD ORDER (recommended phases):
1. Kernel: events.jsonl + plain hash chain + plan hash pin + gate above dispatch + active_run.json. Runner becomes dumb; gate is a single decorated function above runner dispatch.
2. Three step kinds: code, attested, nested. (code is a strict superset of today's python+command runtime kinds.)
3. produces-with-inline-checks; repeat:{until} and repeat:{for_each} on bodies.
4. Authoring: Python DSL + verify helpers + author check + author describe + author new.
5. Lifecycle verbs split by audience as above.
6. Stop-hook nudge with mandatory prohibition preamble.
7. CAS per-project (under <slug>/.cas/), symlink-based produces.
8. Inbox surface for external completion signals.
9. Author test with golden runs.

EXISTING REPO STATE:
- artagents/core/orchestrator/ exists with schema.py, runner.py, registry.py, cli.py, api.py. Currently knows ORCHESTRATOR_KINDS={built_in, external} and RUNTIME_KINDS={python, command}. OrchestratorPlan / OrchestratorPlanStep skeletons exist (used for dry-run output).
- artagents/core/project/ exists with run lifecycle (prepare_project_run / finalize_project_run), paths.py with DEFAULT_PROJECTS_ROOT.
- artagents/threads/ exists with thread_wrapper around runs, @active concept.
- artagents/packs/ holds builtin packs (cut, transcribe, human_notes, open_in_reigh, etc.).
- Migration to packs is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13).

ASSUMED OPEN-CALL DEFAULTS (the design plan should call these out and confirm or revise):
- Keep nested step kind as data (gate sees the structure); forbid code-step-calls-orchestrator.
- Python DSL is the only committed representation; JSON manifest is a build artifact in gitignored build/ dirs.
- Drop .index.db from v1; ship with file-walk verbs.

DELIVERABLE EXPECTATIONS for the design doc:
- Concrete phasing aligned to build order 1-9 above; each phase has scope, files touched, breaking-change risk classification (additive vs breaking), and exit criteria.
- Test strategy at each phase (golden runs are the regression format).
- Migration of one builtin pack (suggest hype.cut or transcribe) to the new shape as the canonical example, including the diff shape.
- Documentation deliverables identified: AUTHORING.md (one-page reference for both human and LLM authors), AGENT.md template, README.md updates.
- Risk register (single-host assumption, semantic verifier discipline, context decay re-injection, agent honor model boundary).
- Cut points where work can stop and still ship something useful (e.g., after phase 3, after phase 5).
- A ## Settled Decisions section at the bottom listing every load-bearing design call (SD-NNN format) so future code-mode runs can inherit them via --from-doc.

{
```json
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md\n@@\n There is no `.index.db` in V1. Status, audit, and author-test tooling should derive state by reading `active_run.json`, validating the `events.jsonl` chain, and walking files under the run directory.\n+\n+## 4. Step Kinds\n+\n+V1 has exactly three task-mode step kinds.\n+\n+`code` is a deterministic argv subprocess. The runner records argv, cwd, env, returncode, stdout/stderr references, and declared `produces`. It is a strict superset of today's `RUNTIME_KINDS={python, command}` in `artagents/core/orchestrator/schema.py`: current `python` and `command` orchestrator runtimes keep loading, while compiled task plans normalize executable work to argv. A `code` step may target the existing executor surface, for example `python3 -m artagents executors run builtin.transcribe ...`. A `code` step must not target `artagents orchestrators run`; sub-orchestrator delegation is represented only by `nested`.\n+\n+`attested` is an agent or human task. It carries instructions, declared `produces`, attestor type, and acknowledgment requirements. Agent attestations require `--agent <id>`. Human attestations require `--actor <name>` matching `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`. Self-acks and unpinned actors are rejected because the evidence must name a distinct accountable identity.\n+\n+`nested` delegates to a child plan. The child plan is data inside the compiled tree, not a hidden process call. The gate sees the nested structure for hashing, cursor derivation, and pinning before any child body runs.\n+\n+## 5. Iteration & Fan-Out\n+\n+Iteration and fan-out are body attributes, not step kinds. Either form can wrap one step or a nested body:\n+\n+```json\n+{\"repeat\": {\"until\": \"user_approves\", \"max_iterations\": 3, \"on_exhaust\": \"abort\"}}\n+{\"repeat\": {\"for_each\": \"<input_set>\"}}\n+```\n+\n+`repeat.until` supports `user_approves`, `verifier_passes`, and `quorum`. Each attempt writes under `steps/<step-id>/iterations/NNN/`, with feedback accumulated into the next iteration constraints. Exhaustion follows `on_exhaust`.\n+\n+`repeat.for_each` expands a body over an input set and writes per-item state under `steps/<step-id>/items/<item-id>/`. `ack --item <id>` targets a single fan-out item, so partial approval is supported without advancing unfinished siblings.\n+\n+## 6. Produces & Inline Checks\n+\n+`produces` declares artifacts and semantic checks inline with the step that creates or attests them:\n+\n+```python\n+produces = {\n+    \"audio\": file(path=\"audio.wav\", check=audio_duration_min(30)),\n+    \"notes\": json_file(path=\"notes.json\", schema=\"notes.schema.json\", min_items=3),\n+}\n+```\n+\n+The V1 verifier helper surface is `artagents.verify`: `file_nonempty`, `json_schema`, `json_file`, `audio_duration_min`, `image_dimensions`, and `all_of`. The gate runs these checks after a step completes or is acknowledged. A failed check records the failure and rewinds the cursor to the same body. `passes_test` is not special syntax; it remains a normal `code` step that runs the requested test argv. Sentinel-existence-only checks are rejected at definition time for attested steps with non-trivial `produces`, because they do not prove the artifact satisfies the contract.\n*** End Patch\n"
}
```

> DEVELOPER

Author the planned document sections.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents

        Output path (write all sections here):
        docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md

        Idea:
ArtAgents is a file-based, single-host Python CLI for running creative pipelines (video/audio/image) that mix code, AI, and human steps. Today it has a pack-based plugin architecture, orchestrator + executor schemas (python/command runtime kinds only), project runs under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/, threads with provenance, and a CLI gateway. We are extending it so an agent can be put into 'task mode' and walked through a frozen plan that mixes code, AI, and human steps, with iteration loops and fan-out.

NON-NEGOTIABLES:
- Single-host, file-based. No daemon, no web server.
- Honor-based with strong logging. We do not pretend to sandbox a misaligned agent.
- Migration is additive. Existing JSON manifests and python/command runtime kinds keep working.
- Existing pack mechanism stays.

V1 DESIGN (post-critique, trimmed):

THREE STEP KINDS:
- code: deterministic argv, runner subprocesses, returncode + produces.
- attested: agent or human task. instructions + produces + ack records identity (agent_id or actor pinned via ARTAGENTS_ACTOR) + evidence.
- nested: delegates to a child plan; gate sees the structure for hashing/pinning.

ITERATION AND FAN-OUT ARE BODY ATTRIBUTES, NOT KINDS:
- repeat: {until: user_approves|verifier_passes|quorum, max_iterations, on_exhaust}
- repeat: {for_each: <input_set>}
Both apply to any step or to a body block in a nested plan.

PRODUCES WITH INLINE CHECKS (replaces separate verifier substep):
  produces = {
    'audio': file(path='audio.wav', check=audio_duration_min(30)),
    'notes': json_file(path='notes.json', schema='notes.schema.json', min_items=3),
  }
The gate runs the check; failure rewinds the cursor. passes_test is a regular code step.

STORAGE (under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/):
- <slug>/active_run.json -- pointer to in-flight run + plan hash
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/plan.json -- immutable, hash-pinned (renamed from 'checklist' since it's a tree)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/events.jsonl -- append-only, plain hash-chained (prev_hash field), sole truth
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md -- contract for whoever's driving
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/ -- artifact files (or symlinks into .cas)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/iterations/NNN/ -- when repeat:until is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/items/<item-id>/ -- when repeat:for_each is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/inbox/ -- external completion signals get dropped here
- <slug>/.cas/<sha256> -- per-project CAS for v1 (not shared)

NO crypto: hash chain is plain sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json). Tripwire for accidental edits, not protection against malicious users. We do not need HMAC or key handles.

NO .index.db in v1. find + grep on events.jsonl is fine until grep is slow.

GATE (above dispatch, single function):
- active_run.json exists for this project?
- plan.json hash matches the pin in active_run.json?
- events.jsonl hash chain intact?
- incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command?
If any fails, reject with the exact recovery command.

LIFECYCLE VERBS, scoped by audience:
artagents next | ack | status | abort                                  (agent-facing, mid-run)
artagents start | abort | status | runs ls                             (operator)
artagents author new | check | describe | test | compile | explain     (author)

ACK DECISIONS (intentionally distinct):
--decision approve              -- advance cursor
--decision retry                -- only valid after verifier failure; rerun body, stderr is feedback
--decision iterate --feedback   -- only valid when repeat.until=user_approves; appends to cumulative constraints
--decision abort                -- end run

ACK requires --agent <id> for attested steps with attestor_type=agent, or --actor <name> matching ARTAGENTS_ACTOR for attestor_type=human. Self-acks and unpinned actors are rejected.

For repeat:for_each: --item <id> targets a specific item; partial approval is supported.

NUDGE (out-of-process, optional, Claude Code only for v1):
A Stop hook runs artagents next. The next output ALWAYS includes the prohibition preamble verbatim, not just on first call -- this is the re-injection mechanism that fights context decay.

AUTHORING:
- Python DSL artagents.orchestrate is the ONLY committed representation. JSON manifest is generated at author compile into a build/ dir (gitignored) and that's what the runtime hashes/pins.
- Verifier helper library artagents.verify: file_nonempty, json_schema, json_file, audio_duration_min, image_dimensions, all_of.
- Pack layout: <pack>/<orch>.py (committed), <pack>/build/<orch>.json (gitignored), <pack>/fixtures/<name>/, <pack>/golden/<name>.events.jsonl.
- author check is sub-second static validation: schema, every produces/requires reference resolves, every nested plan resolves, attested steps with non-trivial produces have semantic checks (sentinel-existence-only is rejected at definition time).
- author describe pretty-prints the DAG.
- author test --fixture runs --dry-run --auto-approve, diffs events.jsonl against golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl.

V1 BUILD ORDER (recommended phases):
1. Kernel: events.jsonl + plain hash chain + plan hash pin + gate above dispatch + active_run.json. Runner becomes dumb; gate is a single decorated function above runner dispatch.
2. Three step kinds: code, attested, nested. (code is a strict superset of today's python+command runtime kinds.)
3. produces-with-inline-checks; repeat:{until} and repeat:{for_each} on bodies.
4. Authoring: Python DSL + verify helpers + author check + author describe + author new.
5. Lifecycle verbs split by audience as above.
6. Stop-hook nudge with mandatory prohibition preamble.
7. CAS per-project (under <slug>/.cas/), symlink-based produces.
8. Inbox surface for external completion signals.
9. Author test with golden runs.

EXISTING REPO STATE:
- artagents/core/orchestrator/ exists with schema.py, runner.py, registry.py, cli.py, api.py. Currently knows ORCHESTRATOR_KINDS={built_in, external} and RUNTIME_KINDS={python, command}. OrchestratorPlan / OrchestratorPlanStep skeletons exist (used for dry-run output).
- artagents/core/project/ exists with run lifecycle (prepare_project_run / finalize_project_run), paths.py with DEFAULT_PROJECTS_ROOT.
- artagents/threads/ exists with thread_wrapper around runs, @active concept.
- artagents/packs/ holds builtin packs (cut, transcribe, human_notes, open_in_reigh, etc.).
- Migration to packs is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13).

ASSUMED OPEN-CALL DEFAULTS (the design plan should call these out and confirm or revise):
- Keep nested step kind as data (gate sees the structure); forbid code-step-calls-orchestrator.
- Python DSL is the only committed representation; JSON manifest is a build artifact in gitignored build/ dirs.
- Drop .index.db from v1; ship with file-walk verbs.

DELIVERABLE EXPECTATIONS for the design doc:
- Concrete phasing aligned to build order 1-9 above; each phase has scope, files touched, breaking-change risk classification (additive vs breaking), and exit criteria.
- Test strategy at each phase (golden runs are the regression format).
- Migration of one builtin pack (suggest hype.cut or transcribe) to the new shape as the canonical example, including the diff shape.
- Documentation deliverables identified: AUTHORING.md (one-page reference for both human and LLM authors), AGENT.md template, README.md updates.
- Risk register (single-host assumption, semantic verifier discipline, context decay re-injection, agent honor model boundary).
- Cut points where work can stop and still ship something useful (e.g., after phase 3, after phase 5).
- A ## Settled Decisions section at the bottom listing every load-bearing design call (SD-NNN format) so future code-mode runs can inherit them via --from-doc.

{
```json
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md\n@@\n The V1 verifier helper surface is `artagents.verify`: `file_nonempty`, `json_schema`, `json_file`, `audio_duration_min`, `image_dimensions`, and `all_of`. The gate runs these checks after a step completes or is acknowledged. A failed check records the failure and rewinds the cursor to the same body. `passes_test` is not special syntax; it remains a normal `code` step that runs the requested test argv. Sentinel-existence-only checks are rejected at definition time for attested steps with non-trivial `produces`, because they do not prove the artifact satisfies the contract.\n+\n+## 7. Gate Above Dispatch\n+\n+Task mode has one gate above runner dispatch. It performs four checks before any project-run or thread side effect:\n+\n+1. `active_run.json` exists for the selected project.\n+2. `runs/<run-id>/plan.json` hashes to the `plan_hash` pinned in `active_run.json`.\n+3. `runs/<run-id>/events.jsonl` has an intact hash chain.\n+4. The incoming command matches `plan[derived_cursor].command`.\n+\n+Any failure exits non-zero and prints one exact recovery command. Missing active run points to `artagents start --project <slug> <compiled-plan>`. A hash-chain or plan-pin failure points to `artagents status --project <slug> --explain` followed by the explicit `artagents abort --project <slug> --reason integrity-failure` command. A command mismatch points to `artagents next --project <slug>` so the agent receives the current allowed command again.\n+\n+Ordering is load-bearing. The first gate is invoked from `artagents/pipeline.py:15`, before control can reach `artagents/core/orchestrator/runner.py:run_orchestrator`. A defensive gate decorator at the top of `run_orchestrator()` re-checks before `_prepare_project_request()` at `artagents/core/orchestrator/runner.py:135` and before `thread_wrapper.begin_orchestrator_run()` at `artagents/core/orchestrator/runner.py:136`. Rejection writes zero project-run files.\n+\n+`events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance. `artagents/threads/wrapper.py` keeps existing thread provenance in `runs/<id>/run.json`; task mode does not duplicate those thread records into `events.jsonl`.\n*** End Patch\n"
}
```

> DEVELOPER

Author the planned document sections.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents

        Output path (write all sections here):
        docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md

        Idea:
ArtAgents is a file-based, single-host Python CLI for running creative pipelines (video/audio/image) that mix code, AI, and human steps. Today it has a pack-based plugin architecture, orchestrator + executor schemas (python/command runtime kinds only), project runs under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/, threads with provenance, and a CLI gateway. We are extending it so an agent can be put into 'task mode' and walked through a frozen plan that mixes code, AI, and human steps, with iteration loops and fan-out.

NON-NEGOTIABLES:
- Single-host, file-based. No daemon, no web server.
- Honor-based with strong logging. We do not pretend to sandbox a misaligned agent.
- Migration is additive. Existing JSON manifests and python/command runtime kinds keep working.
- Existing pack mechanism stays.

V1 DESIGN (post-critique, trimmed):

THREE STEP KINDS:
- code: deterministic argv, runner subprocesses, returncode + produces.
- attested: agent or human task. instructions + produces + ack records identity (agent_id or actor pinned via ARTAGENTS_ACTOR) + evidence.
- nested: delegates to a child plan; gate sees the structure for hashing/pinning.

ITERATION AND FAN-OUT ARE BODY ATTRIBUTES, NOT KINDS:
- repeat: {until: user_approves|verifier_passes|quorum, max_iterations, on_exhaust}
- repeat: {for_each: <input_set>}
Both apply to any step or to a body block in a nested plan.

PRODUCES WITH INLINE CHECKS (replaces separate verifier substep):
  produces = {
    'audio': file(path='audio.wav', check=audio_duration_min(30)),
    'notes': json_file(path='notes.json', schema='notes.schema.json', min_items=3),
  }
The gate runs the check; failure rewinds the cursor. passes_test is a regular code step.

STORAGE (under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/):
- <slug>/active_run.json -- pointer to in-flight run + plan hash
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/plan.json -- immutable, hash-pinned (renamed from 'checklist' since it's a tree)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/events.jsonl -- append-only, plain hash-chained (prev_hash field), sole truth
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md -- contract for whoever's driving
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/ -- artifact files (or symlinks into .cas)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/iterations/NNN/ -- when repeat:until is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/items/<item-id>/ -- when repeat:for_each is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/inbox/ -- external completion signals get dropped here
- <slug>/.cas/<sha256> -- per-project CAS for v1 (not shared)

NO crypto: hash chain is plain sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json). Tripwire for accidental edits, not protection against malicious users. We do not need HMAC or key handles.

NO .index.db in v1. find + grep on events.jsonl is fine until grep is slow.

GATE (above dispatch, single function):
- active_run.json exists for this project?
- plan.json hash matches the pin in active_run.json?
- events.jsonl hash chain intact?
- incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command?
If any fails, reject with the exact recovery command.

LIFECYCLE VERBS, scoped by audience:
artagents next | ack | status | abort                                  (agent-facing, mid-run)
artagents start | abort | status | runs ls                             (operator)
artagents author new | check | describe | test | compile | explain     (author)

ACK DECISIONS (intentionally distinct):
--decision approve              -- advance cursor
--decision retry                -- only valid after verifier failure; rerun body, stderr is feedback
--decision iterate --feedback   -- only valid when repeat.until=user_approves; appends to cumulative constraints
--decision abort                -- end run

ACK requires --agent <id> for attested steps with attestor_type=agent, or --actor <name> matching ARTAGENTS_ACTOR for attestor_type=human. Self-acks and unpinned actors are rejected.

For repeat:for_each: --item <id> targets a specific item; partial approval is supported.

NUDGE (out-of-process, optional, Claude Code only for v1):
A Stop hook runs artagents next. The next output ALWAYS includes the prohibition preamble verbatim, not just on first call -- this is the re-injection mechanism that fights context decay.

AUTHORING:
- Python DSL artagents.orchestrate is the ONLY committed representation. JSON manifest is generated at author compile into a build/ dir (gitignored) and that's what the runtime hashes/pins.
- Verifier helper library artagents.verify: file_nonempty, json_schema, json_file, audio_duration_min, image_dimensions, all_of.
- Pack layout: <pack>/<orch>.py (committed), <pack>/build/<orch>.json (gitignored), <pack>/fixtures/<name>/, <pack>/golden/<name>.events.jsonl.
- author check is sub-second static validation: schema, every produces/requires reference resolves, every nested plan resolves, attested steps with non-trivial produces have semantic checks (sentinel-existence-only is rejected at definition time).
- author describe pretty-prints the DAG.
- author test --fixture runs --dry-run --auto-approve, diffs events.jsonl against golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl.

V1 BUILD ORDER (recommended phases):
1. Kernel: events.jsonl + plain hash chain + plan hash pin + gate above dispatch + active_run.json. Runner becomes dumb; gate is a single decorated function above runner dispatch.
2. Three step kinds: code, attested, nested. (code is a strict superset of today's python+command runtime kinds.)
3. produces-with-inline-checks; repeat:{until} and repeat:{for_each} on bodies.
4. Authoring: Python DSL + verify helpers + author check + author describe + author new.
5. Lifecycle verbs split by audience as above.
6. Stop-hook nudge with mandatory prohibition preamble.
7. CAS per-project (under <slug>/.cas/), symlink-based produces.
8. Inbox surface for external completion signals.
9. Author test with golden runs.

EXISTING REPO STATE:
- artagents/core/orchestrator/ exists with schema.py, runner.py, registry.py, cli.py, api.py. Currently knows ORCHESTRATOR_KINDS={built_in, external} and RUNTIME_KINDS={python, command}. OrchestratorPlan / OrchestratorPlanStep skeletons exist (used for dry-run output).
- artagents/core/project/ exists with run lifecycle (prepare_project_run / finalize_project_run), paths.py with DEFAULT_PROJECTS_ROOT.
- artagents/threads/ exists with thread_wrapper around runs, @active concept.
- artagents/packs/ holds builtin packs (cut, transcribe, human_notes, open_in_reigh, etc.).
- Migration to packs is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13).

ASSUMED OPEN-CALL DEFAULTS (the design plan should call these out and confirm or revise):
- Keep nested step kind as data (gate sees the structure); forbid code-step-calls-orchestrator.
- Python DSL is the only committed representation; JSON manifest is a build artifact in gitignored build/ dirs.
- Drop .index.db from v1; ship with file-walk verbs.

DELIVERABLE EXPECTATIONS for the design doc:
- Concrete phasing aligned to build order 1-9 above; each phase has scope, files touched, breaking-change risk classification (additive vs breaking), and exit criteria.
- Test strategy at each phase (golden runs are the regression format).
- Migration of one builtin pack (suggest hype.cut or transcribe) to the new shape as the canonical example, including the diff shape.
- Documentation deliverables identified: AUTHORING.md (one-page reference for both human and LLM authors), AGENT.md template, README.md updates.
- Risk register (single-host assumption, semantic verifier discipline, context decay re-injection, agent honor model boundary).
- Cut points where work can stop and still ship something useful (e.g., after phase 3, after phase 5).
- A ## Settled Decisions section at the bottom listing every load-bearing design call (SD-NNN format) so future code-mode runs can inherit them via --from-doc.

{
```json
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md\n@@\n `events.jsonl` is the single write surface for task-mode provenance. `artagents/threads/wrapper.py` keeps existing thread provenance in `runs/<id>/run.json`; task mode does not duplicate those thread records into `events.jsonl`.\n+\n+## 8. Lifecycle Verbs\n+\n+Task-mode verbs land in the top-level `artagents/pipeline.py:15` dispatch, not under the existing `orchestrators` subparser. `artagents orchestrators run|list|inspect|validate` in `artagents/core/orchestrator/cli.py` remains unchanged for current pack orchestrators.\n+\n+Agent-facing mid-run verbs:\n+\n+- `artagents next`\n+- `artagents ack`\n+- `artagents status`\n+- `artagents abort`\n+\n+Operator verbs:\n+\n+- `artagents start`\n+- `artagents abort`\n+- `artagents status`\n+- `artagents runs ls`\n+\n+Author verbs:\n+\n+- `artagents author new`\n+- `artagents author check`\n+- `artagents author describe`\n+- `artagents author test`\n+- `artagents author compile`\n+- `artagents author explain`\n+\n+The split is an audience boundary, not three runtimes. `next` and `ack` are for the current driver. `start`, `runs ls`, and operator `abort` are for project-run control. `author` commands operate on DSL source and build artifacts before runtime hashing.\n+\n+## 9. Ack Decisions\n+\n+Ack decisions are intentionally distinct:\n+\n+| Decision | Valid when | Effect |\n+| --- | --- | --- |\n+| `--decision approve` | Current step is awaiting approval and inline checks pass. | Advances the cursor. |\n+| `--decision retry` | Current body is stopped after verifier failure. | Reruns the body; stderr and verifier output become feedback. |\n+| `--decision iterate --feedback <text>` | Current body has `repeat.until=user_approves`. | Appends feedback to cumulative constraints and starts the next iteration. |\n+| `--decision abort` | Any in-flight run. | Ends the run and records the abort event. |\n+\n+Identity is required for attested steps. `attestor_type=agent` requires `--agent <id>`. `attestor_type=human` requires `--actor <name>` and the name must match `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`. Self-acks and unpinned actors are rejected. For `repeat.for_each`, `--item <id>` targets one item, enabling partial approval without approving the whole fan-out set.\n*** End Patch\n"
}
```

> DEVELOPER

Author the planned document sections.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents

        Output path (write all sections here):
        docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md

        Idea:
ArtAgents is a file-based, single-host Python CLI for running creative pipelines (video/audio/image) that mix code, AI, and human steps. Today it has a pack-based plugin architecture, orchestrator + executor schemas (python/command runtime kinds only), project runs under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/, threads with provenance, and a CLI gateway. We are extending it so an agent can be put into 'task mode' and walked through a frozen plan that mixes code, AI, and human steps, with iteration loops and fan-out.

NON-NEGOTIABLES:
- Single-host, file-based. No daemon, no web server.
- Honor-based with strong logging. We do not pretend to sandbox a misaligned agent.
- Migration is additive. Existing JSON manifests and python/command runtime kinds keep working.
- Existing pack mechanism stays.

V1 DESIGN (post-critique, trimmed):

THREE STEP KINDS:
- code: deterministic argv, runner subprocesses, returncode + produces.
- attested: agent or human task. instructions + produces + ack records identity (agent_id or actor pinned via ARTAGENTS_ACTOR) + evidence.
- nested: delegates to a child plan; gate sees the structure for hashing/pinning.

ITERATION AND FAN-OUT ARE BODY ATTRIBUTES, NOT KINDS:
- repeat: {until: user_approves|verifier_passes|quorum, max_iterations, on_exhaust}
- repeat: {for_each: <input_set>}
Both apply to any step or to a body block in a nested plan.

PRODUCES WITH INLINE CHECKS (replaces separate verifier substep):
  produces = {
    'audio': file(path='audio.wav', check=audio_duration_min(30)),
    'notes': json_file(path='notes.json', schema='notes.schema.json', min_items=3),
  }
The gate runs the check; failure rewinds the cursor. passes_test is a regular code step.

STORAGE (under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/):
- <slug>/active_run.json -- pointer to in-flight run + plan hash
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/plan.json -- immutable, hash-pinned (renamed from 'checklist' since it's a tree)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/events.jsonl -- append-only, plain hash-chained (prev_hash field), sole truth
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md -- contract for whoever's driving
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/ -- artifact files (or symlinks into .cas)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/iterations/NNN/ -- when repeat:until is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/items/<item-id>/ -- when repeat:for_each is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/inbox/ -- external completion signals get dropped here
- <slug>/.cas/<sha256> -- per-project CAS for v1 (not shared)

NO crypto: hash chain is plain sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json). Tripwire for accidental edits, not protection against malicious users. We do not need HMAC or key handles.

NO .index.db in v1. find + grep on events.jsonl is fine until grep is slow.

GATE (above dispatch, single function):
- active_run.json exists for this project?
- plan.json hash matches the pin in active_run.json?
- events.jsonl hash chain intact?
- incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command?
If any fails, reject with the exact recovery command.

LIFECYCLE VERBS, scoped by audience:
artagents next | ack | status | abort                                  (agent-facing, mid-run)
artagents start | abort | status | runs ls                             (operator)
artagents author new | check | describe | test | compile | explain     (author)

ACK DECISIONS (intentionally distinct):
--decision approve              -- advance cursor
--decision retry                -- only valid after verifier failure; rerun body, stderr is feedback
--decision iterate --feedback   -- only valid when repeat.until=user_approves; appends to cumulative constraints
--decision abort                -- end run

ACK requires --agent <id> for attested steps with attestor_type=agent, or --actor <name> matching ARTAGENTS_ACTOR for attestor_type=human. Self-acks and unpinned actors are rejected.

For repeat:for_each: --item <id> targets a specific item; partial approval is supported.

NUDGE (out-of-process, optional, Claude Code only for v1):
A Stop hook runs artagents next. The next output ALWAYS includes the prohibition preamble verbatim, not just on first call -- this is the re-injection mechanism that fights context decay.

AUTHORING:
- Python DSL artagents.orchestrate is the ONLY committed representation. JSON manifest is generated at author compile into a build/ dir (gitignored) and that's what the runtime hashes/pins.
- Verifier helper library artagents.verify: file_nonempty, json_schema, json_file, audio_duration_min, image_dimensions, all_of.
- Pack layout: <pack>/<orch>.py (committed), <pack>/build/<orch>.json (gitignored), <pack>/fixtures/<name>/, <pack>/golden/<name>.events.jsonl.
- author check is sub-second static validation: schema, every produces/requires reference resolves, every nested plan resolves, attested steps with non-trivial produces have semantic checks (sentinel-existence-only is rejected at definition time).
- author describe pretty-prints the DAG.
- author test --fixture runs --dry-run --auto-approve, diffs events.jsonl against golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl.

V1 BUILD ORDER (recommended phases):
1. Kernel: events.jsonl + plain hash chain + plan hash pin + gate above dispatch + active_run.json. Runner becomes dumb; gate is a single decorated function above runner dispatch.
2. Three step kinds: code, attested, nested. (code is a strict superset of today's python+command runtime kinds.)
3. produces-with-inline-checks; repeat:{until} and repeat:{for_each} on bodies.
4. Authoring: Python DSL + verify helpers + author check + author describe + author new.
5. Lifecycle verbs split by audience as above.
6. Stop-hook nudge with mandatory prohibition preamble.
7. CAS per-project (under <slug>/.cas/), symlink-based produces.
8. Inbox surface for external completion signals.
9. Author test with golden runs.

EXISTING REPO STATE:
- artagents/core/orchestrator/ exists with schema.py, runner.py, registry.py, cli.py, api.py. Currently knows ORCHESTRATOR_KINDS={built_in, external} and RUNTIME_KINDS={python, command}. OrchestratorPlan / OrchestratorPlanStep skeletons exist (used for dry-run output).
- artagents/core/project/ exists with run lifecycle (prepare_project_run / finalize_project_run), paths.py with DEFAULT_PROJECTS_ROOT.
- artagents/threads/ exists with thread_wrapper around runs, @active concept.
- artagents/packs/ holds builtin packs (cut, transcribe, human_notes, open_in_reigh, etc.).
- Migration to packs is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13).

ASSUMED OPEN-CALL DEFAULTS (the design plan should call these out and confirm or revise):
- Keep nested step kind as data (gate sees the structure); forbid code-step-calls-orchestrator.
- Python DSL is the only committed representation; JSON manifest is a build artifact in gitignored build/ dirs.
- Drop .index.db from v1; ship with file-walk verbs.

DELIVERABLE EXPECTATIONS for the design doc:
- Concrete phasing aligned to build order 1-9 above; each phase has scope, files touched, breaking-change risk classification (additive vs breaking), and exit criteria.
- Test strategy at each phase (golden runs are the regression format).
- Migration of one builtin pack (suggest hype.cut or transcribe) to the new shape as the canonical example, including the diff shape.
- Documentation deliverables identified: AUTHORING.md (one-page reference for both human and LLM authors), AGENT.md template, README.md updates.
- Risk register (single-host assumption, semantic verifier discipline, context decay re-injection, agent honor model boundary).
- Cut points where work can stop and still ship something useful (e.g., after phase 3, after phase 5).
- A ## Settled Decisions section at the bottom listing every load-bearing design call (SD-NNN format) so future code-mode runs can inherit them via --from-doc.

{
```json
  "patch": "*** Begin Patch\n*** Update File: docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md\n@@\n Identity is required for attested steps. `attestor_type=agent` requires `--agent <id>`. `attestor_type=human` requires `--actor <name>` and the name must match `ARTAGENTS_ACTOR`. Self-acks and unpinned actors are rejected. For `repeat.for_each`, `--item <id>` targets one item, enabling partial approval without approving the whole fan-out set.\n+\n+## 10. Authoring\n+\n+`artagents.orchestrate` is the only committed representation for new task-mode plans. JSON is a generated build artifact: `artagents author compile` writes `<pack>/build/<orch>.json`, that path is gitignored, and runtime hashing pins the compiled artifact rather than the Python source.\n+\n+Pack additions are additive over the current `artagents/packs/builtin/hype/` shape:\n+\n+- `<pack>/<orch>.py` for committed DSL source.\n+- `<pack>/build/<orch>.json` for gitignored compiled JSON.\n+- `<pack>/fixtures/<name>/` for author-test inputs.\n+- `<pack>/golden/<name>.events.jsonl` for golden regression output.\n+\n+`artagents.verify` provides the shared check helpers: `file_nonempty`, `json_schema`, `json_file`, `audio_duration_min`, `image_dimensions`, and `all_of`.\n+\n+`artagents author check` is sub-second static validation. It validates schema shape, verifies every `produces` and `requires` reference resolves, verifies every nested plan resolves, rejects `code` step argv targeting `artagents orchestrators run`, and requires semantic checks for attested steps with non-trivial `produces`. Sentinel-only checks are rejected. `artagents author describe` pretty-prints the DAG. `artagents author test --fixture <name>` runs `--dry-run --auto-approve` and diffs the resulting `events.jsonl` against `<pack>/golden/<name>.events.jsonl`.\n+\n+## 11. Stop-Hook Nudge\n+\n+The V1 nudge is out-of-process and Claude Code only. A Stop hook runs `artagents next` for the active task-mode run. Every `artagents next` response includes the prohibition preamble verbatim, including after the first call. This is the context-decay countermeasure: the driver is repeatedly reminded that only the printed command is allowed, that self-acks are rejected, and that manual side work outside the plan will not advance the cursor.\n*** End Patch\n"
}
```

        Idea:
ArtAgents is a file-based, single-host Python CLI for running creative pipelines (video/audio/image) that mix code, AI, and human steps. Today it has a pack-based plugin architecture, orchestrator + executor schemas (python/command runtime kinds only), project runs under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/, threads with provenance, and a CLI gateway. We are extending it so an agent can be put into 'task mode' and walked through a frozen plan that mixes code, AI, and human steps, with iteration loops and fan-out.

NON-NEGOTIABLES:
- Single-host, file-based. No daemon, no web server.
- Honor-based with strong logging. We do not pretend to sandbox a misaligned agent.
- Migration is additive. Existing JSON manifests and python/command runtime kinds keep working.
- Existing pack mechanism stays.

V1 DESIGN (post-critique, trimmed):

THREE STEP KINDS:
- code: deterministic argv, runner subprocesses, returncode + produces.
- attested: agent or human task. instructions + produces + ack records identity (agent_id or actor pinned via ARTAGENTS_ACTOR) + evidence.
- nested: delegates to a child plan; gate sees the structure for hashing/pinning.

ITERATION AND FAN-OUT ARE BODY ATTRIBUTES, NOT KINDS:
- repeat: {until: user_approves|verifier_passes|quorum, max_iterations, on_exhaust}
- repeat: {for_each: <input_set>}
Both apply to any step or to a body block in a nested plan.

PRODUCES WITH INLINE CHECKS (replaces separate verifier substep):
  produces = {
    'audio': file(path='audio.wav', check=audio_duration_min(30)),
    'notes': json_file(path='notes.json', schema='notes.schema.json', min_items=3),
  }
The gate runs the check; failure rewinds the cursor. passes_test is a regular code step.

STORAGE (under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/):
- <slug>/active_run.json -- pointer to in-flight run + plan hash
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/plan.json -- immutable, hash-pinned (renamed from 'checklist' since it's a tree)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/events.jsonl -- append-only, plain hash-chained (prev_hash field), sole truth
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md -- contract for whoever's driving
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/ -- artifact files (or symlinks into .cas)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/iterations/NNN/ -- when repeat:until is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/items/<item-id>/ -- when repeat:for_each is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/inbox/ -- external completion signals get dropped here
- <slug>/.cas/<sha256> -- per-project CAS for v1 (not shared)

NO crypto: hash chain is plain sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json). Tripwire for accidental edits, not protection against malicious users. We do not need HMAC or key handles.

NO .index.db in v1. find + grep on events.jsonl is fine until grep is slow.

GATE (above dispatch, single function):
- active_run.json exists for this project?
- plan.json hash matches the pin in active_run.json?
- events.jsonl hash chain intact?
- incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command?
If any fails, reject with the exact recovery command.

LIFECYCLE VERBS, scoped by audience:
artagents next | ack | status | abort                                  (agent-facing, mid-run)
artagents start | abort | status | runs ls                             (operator)
artagents author new | check | describe | test | compile | explain     (author)

ACK DECISIONS (intentionally distinct):
--decision approve              -- advance cursor
--decision retry                -- only valid after verifier failure; rerun body, stderr is feedback
--decision iterate --feedback   -- only valid when repeat.until=user_approves; appends to cumulative constraints
--decision abort                -- end run

ACK requires --agent <id> for attested steps with attestor_type=agent, or --actor <name> matching ARTAGENTS_ACTOR for attestor_type=human. Self-acks and unpinned actors are rejected.

For repeat:for_each: --item <id> targets a specific item; partial approval is supported.

NUDGE (out-of-process, optional, Claude Code only for v1):
A Stop hook runs artagents next. The next output ALWAYS includes the prohibition preamble verbatim, not just on first call -- this is the re-injection mechanism that fights context decay.

AUTHORING:
- Python DSL artagents.orchestrate is the ONLY committed representation. JSON manifest is generated at author compile into a build/ dir (gitignored) and that's what the runtime hashes/pins.
- Verifier helper library artagents.verify: file_nonempty, json_schema, json_file, audio_duration_min, image_dimensions, all_of.
- Pack layout: <pack>/<orch>.py (committed), <pack>/build/<orch>.json (gitignored), <pack>/fixtures/<name>/, <pack>/golden/<name>.events.jsonl.
- author check is sub-second static validation: schema, every produces/requires reference resolves, every nested plan resolves, attested steps with non-trivial produces have semantic checks (sentinel-existence-only is rejected at definition time).
- author describe pretty-prints the DAG.
- author test --fixture runs --dry-run --auto-approve, diffs events.jsonl against golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl.

V1 BUILD ORDER (recommended phases):
1. Kernel: events.jsonl + plain hash chain + plan hash pin + gate above dispatch + active_run.json. Runner becomes dumb; gate is a single decorated function above runner dispatch.
2. Three step kinds: code, attested, nested. (code is a strict superset of today's python+command runtime kinds.)
3. produces-with-inline-checks; repeat:{until} and repeat:{for_each} on bodies.
4. Authoring: Python DSL + verify helpers + author check + author describe + author new.
5. Lifecycle verbs split by audience as above.
6. Stop-hook nudge with mandatory prohibition preamble.
7. CAS per-project (under <slug>/.cas/), symlink-based produces.
8. Inbox surface for external completion signals.
9. Author test with golden runs.

EXISTING REPO STATE:
- artagents/core/orchestrator/ exists with schema.py, runner.py, registry.py, cli.py, api.py. Currently knows ORCHESTRATOR_KINDS={built_in, external} and RUNTIME_KINDS={python, command}. OrchestratorPlan / OrchestratorPlanStep skeletons exist (used for dry-run output).
- artagents/core/project/ exists with run lifecycle (prepare_project_run / finalize_project_run), paths.py with DEFAULT_PROJECTS_ROOT.
- artagents/threads/ exists with thread_wrapper around runs, @active concept.
- artagents/packs/ holds builtin packs (cut, transcribe, human_notes, open_in_reigh, etc.).
- Migration to packs is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13).

ASSUMED OPEN-CALL DEFAULTS (the design plan should call these out and confirm or revise):
- Keep nested step kind as data (gate sees the structure); forbid code-step-calls-orchestrator.
- Python DSL is the only committed representation; JSON manifest is a build artifact in gitignored build/ dirs.
- Drop .index.db from v1; ship with file-walk verbs.

DELIVERABLE EXPECTATIONS for the design doc:
- Concrete phasing aligned to build order 1-9 above; each phase has scope, files touched, breaking-change risk classification (additive vs breaking), and exit criteria.
- Test strategy at each phase (golden runs are the regression format).
- Migration of one builtin pack (suggest hype.cut or transcribe) to the new shape as the canonical example, including the diff shape.
- Documentation deliverables identified: AUTHORING.md (one-page reference for both human and LLM authors), AGENT.md template, README.md updates.
- Risk register (single-host assumption, semantic verifier discipline, context decay re-injection, agent honor model boundary).
- Cut points where work can stop and still ship something useful (e.g., after phase 3, after phase 5).
- A ## Settled Decisions section at the bottom listing every load-bearing design call (SD-NNN format) so future code-mode runs can inherit them via --from-doc.

> DEVELOPER

Author the planned document sections.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/ArtAgents

        Output path (write all sections here):
        docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md

        Idea:
ArtAgents is a file-based, single-host Python CLI for running creative pipelines (video/audio/image) that mix code, AI, and human steps. Today it has a pack-based plugin architecture, orchestrator + executor schemas (python/command runtime kinds only), project runs under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/, threads with provenance, and a CLI gateway. We are extending it so an agent can be put into 'task mode' and walked through a frozen plan that mixes code, AI, and human steps, with iteration loops and fan-out.

NON-NEGOTIABLES:
- Single-host, file-based. No daemon, no web server.
- Honor-based with strong logging. We do not pretend to sandbox a misaligned agent.
- Migration is additive. Existing JSON manifests and python/command runtime kinds keep working.
- Existing pack mechanism stays.

V1 DESIGN (post-critique, trimmed):

THREE STEP KINDS:
- code: deterministic argv, runner subprocesses, returncode + produces.
- attested: agent or human task. instructions + produces + ack records identity (agent_id or actor pinned via ARTAGENTS_ACTOR) + evidence.
- nested: delegates to a child plan; gate sees the structure for hashing/pinning.

ITERATION AND FAN-OUT ARE BODY ATTRIBUTES, NOT KINDS:
- repeat: {until: user_approves|verifier_passes|quorum, max_iterations, on_exhaust}
- repeat: {for_each: <input_set>}
Both apply to any step or to a body block in a nested plan.

PRODUCES WITH INLINE CHECKS (replaces separate verifier substep):
  produces = {
    'audio': file(path='audio.wav', check=audio_duration_min(30)),
    'notes': json_file(path='notes.json', schema='notes.schema.json', min_items=3),
  }
The gate runs the check; failure rewinds the cursor. passes_test is a regular code step.

STORAGE (under ~/Documents/reigh-workspace/artagents-projects/):
- <slug>/active_run.json -- pointer to in-flight run + plan hash
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/plan.json -- immutable, hash-pinned (renamed from 'checklist' since it's a tree)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/events.jsonl -- append-only, plain hash-chained (prev_hash field), sole truth
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/AGENT.md -- contract for whoever's driving
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/produces/ -- artifact files (or symlinks into .cas)
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/iterations/NNN/ -- when repeat:until is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/steps/<step-id>/items/<item-id>/ -- when repeat:for_each is set
- <slug>/runs/<run-id>/inbox/ -- external completion signals get dropped here
- <slug>/.cas/<sha256> -- per-project CAS for v1 (not shared)

NO crypto: hash chain is plain sha256(prev_hash + canonical_event_json). Tripwire for accidental edits, not protection against malicious users. We do not need HMAC or key handles.

NO .index.db in v1. find + grep on events.jsonl is fine until grep is slow.

GATE (above dispatch, single function):
- active_run.json exists for this project?
- plan.json hash matches the pin in active_run.json?
- events.jsonl hash chain intact?
- incoming command matches plan[derived_cursor].command?
If any fails, reject with the exact recovery command.

LIFECYCLE VERBS, scoped by audience:
artagents next | ack | status | abort                                  (agent-facing, mid-run)
artagents start | abort | status | runs ls                             (operator)
artagents author new | check | describe | test | compile | explain     (author)

ACK DECISIONS (intentionally distinct):
--decision approve              -- advance cursor
--decision retry                -- only valid after verifier failure; rerun body, stderr is feedback
--decision iterate --feedback   -- only valid when repeat.until=user_approves; appends to cumulative constraints
--decision abort                -- end run

ACK requires --agent <id> for attested steps with attestor_type=agent, or --actor <name> matching ARTAGENTS_ACTOR for attestor_type=human. Self-acks and unpinned actors are rejected.

For repeat:for_each: --item <id> targets a specific item; partial approval is supported.

NUDGE (out-of-process, optional, Claude Code only for v1):
A Stop hook runs artagents next. The next output ALWAYS includes the prohibition preamble verbatim, not just on first call -- this is the re-injection mechanism that fights context decay.

AUTHORING:
- Python DSL artagents.orchestrate is the ONLY committed representation. JSON manifest is generated at author compile into a build/ dir (gitignored) and that's what the runtime hashes/pins.
- Verifier helper library artagents.verify: file_nonempty, json_schema, json_file, audio_duration_min, image_dimensions, all_of.
- Pack layout: <pack>/<orch>.py (committed), <pack>/build/<orch>.json (gitignored), <pack>/fixtures/<name>/, <pack>/golden/<name>.events.jsonl.
- author check is sub-second static validation: schema, every produces/requires reference resolves, every nested plan resolves, attested steps with non-trivial produces have semantic checks (sentinel-existence-only is rejected at definition time).
- author describe pretty-prints the DAG.
- author test --fixture runs --dry-run --auto-approve, diffs events.jsonl against golden/<fixture>.events.jsonl.

V1 BUILD ORDER (recommended phases):
1. Kernel: events.jsonl + plain hash chain + plan hash pin + gate above dispatch + active_run.json. Runner becomes dumb; gate is a single decorated function above runner dispatch.
2. Three step kinds: code, attested, nested. (code is a strict superset of today's python+command runtime kinds.)
3. produces-with-inline-checks; repeat:{until} and repeat:{for_each} on bodies.
4. Authoring: Python DSL + verify helpers + author check + author describe + author new.
5. Lifecycle verbs split by audience as above.
6. Stop-hook nudge with mandatory prohibition preamble.
7. CAS per-project (under <slug>/.cas/), symlink-based produces.
8. Inbox surface for external completion signals.
9. Author test with golden runs.

EXISTING REPO STATE:
- artagents/core/orchestrator/ exists with schema.py, runner.py, registry.py, cli.py, api.py. Currently knows ORCHESTRATOR_KINDS={built_in, external} and RUNTIME_KINDS={python, command}. OrchestratorPlan / OrchestratorPlanStep skeletons exist (used for dry-run output).
- artagents/core/project/ exists with run lifecycle (prepare_project_run / finalize_project_run), paths.py with DEFAULT_PROJECTS_ROOT.
- artagents/threads/ exists with thread_wrapper around runs, @active concept.
- artagents/packs/ holds builtin packs (cut, transcribe, human_notes, open_in_reigh, etc.).
- Migration to packs is mid-flight (recent commits T10-T13).

ASSUMED OPEN-CALL DEFAULTS (the design plan should call these out and confirm or revise):
- Keep nested step kind as data (gate sees the structure); forbid code-step-calls-orchestrator.
- Python DSL is the only committed representation; JSON manifest is a build artifact in gitignored build/ dirs.
- Drop .index.db from v1; ship with file-walk verbs.

DELIVERABLE EXPECTATIONS for the design doc:
- Concrete phasing aligned to build order 1-9 above; each phase has scope, files touched, breaking-change risk classification (additive vs breaking), and exit criteria.
- Test strategy at each phase (golden runs are the regression format).
- Migration of one builtin pack (suggest hype.cut or transcribe) to the new shape as the canonical example, including the diff shape.
- Documentation deliverables identified: AUTHORING.md (one-page reference for both human and LLM authors), AGENT.md template, README.md updates.
- Risk register (single-host assumption, semantic verifier discipline, context decay re-injection, agent honor model boundary).
- Cut points where work can stop and still ship something useful (e.g., after phase 3, after phase 5).
- A ## Settled Decisions section at the bottom listing every load-bearing design call (SD-NNN format) so future code-mode runs can inherit them via --from-doc.

Mode: doc / metaplan
Output: docs/orchestrator-v1-plan.md

User notes and answers:
- strict-notes auto-enabled for metaplan/doc mode
